104. New.RealPlace

New.RealPlace.FrameNorm

Theorem 104.1src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: continuous_frameVector
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $a \in \FinMACRoFDI {(\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)}$. Then the function that maps $x_{2}$ to $\frameVectorMACRoDWI {x_{2}}{a}$ is continuous.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Theorem 104.2src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: frameVector_mul
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $y \in \SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{6}})}$. Let $s \in \SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{5}})}$ such that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\MaxmaxMACRoEC {\ShapeFormbesselUMACRoCQP}{\ShapeFormbesselHMACRoCQM}}{s}$. Then $\frameVectorpartMACRoGRY {(s \times y)}$ is equal to $\frameVectorpartMACRoGRY {y}$.
Proof. By frameVector
and 6 lemmas of Mathlib PiLp.ext, inf_le_right, Matrix.mul_apply, InvMemClass.inv_mem, Subgroup.comap_mono, sup_le
. ∎
Theorem 104.3src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: frameVector_eq_sum
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $a \in \FinMACRoFDI {(\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)}$. Let $y \in \SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{5}})}$. Let $s \in \SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{4}})}$ such that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormbesselUMACRoCQO}{s}$. Then $\frameVectorMACRoDWI {y}{a}$ is equal to $\FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{b}{(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {(\frameMatrixappMACRoGSP {s}{b}{a})}{(\frameVectorMACRoDWI {(s \times y)}{b})})}$.
Proof. By submatrix_mul_besselFrame, frameVector, frameMatrix
and 3 lemmas of Mathlib PiLp.ext, Matrix.mul_apply, mul_comm
. ∎
Theorem 104.4src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: norm_frameVector_le
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $a \in \FinMACRoFDI {(\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)}$. Let $y \in \SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{2}})}$. Then $\NormnormMACRoAK {(\frameVectorMACRoDWI {y}{a})}\leq \NormnorminMACRoFCP {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {y}}$.
Proof. By U, frameVector, U.norm_inv
and 1 lemma of Mathlib Matrix.l2_opNorm_mulVec
. ∎
Theorem 104.5src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: norm_sum_le_mul_norm_sum_frameVector
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $y \in \SubtypeSet {x_{7}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{7}})}$. Let $s \in \SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{6}})}$. Let $I$ be a finite type. Let $e$ be a function from $I$ to $\FinMACRoFDI {(\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)}$. Let $c$ be a function from $I$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $i \in \ShapeBesselIndexMACRoCQI {x}$. Then $\NormnormMACRoAK {(\FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{a}{(c (a)\times \UnitsvalappMACRoHCH {s}{i}{(\ShapebesselFrameMACRoCRC {x}{e (a)})})})}\leq \NormnorminMACRoFCP {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {(s \times y)}}\times \NormnormMACRoAK {(\FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{a_{1}}{(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {c (a_{1})}{(\frameVectorMACRoDWI {y}{e (a_{1})})})})}$.
Proof. By frameVector
and 4 lemmas of Mathlib Matrix.l2_opNorm_mulVec, Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.mulVec_sum, PiLp.norm_apply_le
. ∎
Theorem 104.6src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: besselSize_le
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $y \in \SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{5}})}$. Let $s \in \SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{4}})}$ such that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormbesselSMACRoCQN}{s}$. Then $\besselSizeMACRoDKX {y}\leq \NormnorminMACRoFCP {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {(s \times y)}}+ 1$.
Proof.
By 1 lemma of Mathlib csInf_le
. ∎
Theorem 104.7src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: exists_norm_le_of_besselSize_le
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $y \in \SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{5}})}$. Let $T \in \Real$ such that $\besselSizeMACRoDKX {y}\leq T$. Then there exists an element $u$ of $\SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {(\ShapemiddleGramMACRoCRR {x}{1})}}{x_{4}})}$, such that $u$ is an element of $\ShapeFormbesselUMACRoCQO$ and $\NormnorminMACRoFCP {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {(u \times y)}}\leq T$.
If `besselSize` y ≤ T, then some u in the Bessel unipotent group 𝖴 has operator norm ‖uy‖ ≤ T.
Proof. By exists_besselU_mul_toBesselH, norm_embedH_mul, embedH
and 5 lemmas of Mathlib exists_lt_of_ciInf_lt, lt_add_one, mul_assoc, LE.le.trans_lt, inv_eq_iff_eq_inv
. ∎
Theorem 104.8src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: besselSize_besselS_mul
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $y \in \SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{4}})}$. Let $s \in \SubtypeSet {x_{3}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{3}})}$ such that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormbesselSMACRoCQN}{s}$. Then $\besselSizeMACRoDKX {(s \times y)}= \besselSizeMACRoDKX {y}$.
Proof.
By 2 lemmas of Mathlib mul_assoc, Equiv.iInf_congr
. ∎
Theorem 104.9src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: besselSize_le_norm_mul
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $x_{1} \in \SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{5}})}$. Let $y' \in \SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{4}})}$. Then $\besselSizeMACRoDKX {y'}\leq \NormnorminMACRoFCP {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {(\InvinvMACRoFW {x_{1}}\times y')}}\times \besselSizeMACRoDKX {x_{1}}$.
Proof. By besselSize_le, U.one_le_norm
and 8 lemmas of Mathlib add_mul, Eq.ge, mul_assoc, Real.mul_iInf_of_nonneg, le_ciInf, mul_comm, LE.le.trans, norm_mul_le
. ∎
Theorem 104.10src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: continuous_besselSize
Let $x$ be a shape. Then $\besselSizepartMACRoGRW$ is continuous.
Proof. By besselSize_le_norm_mul, Continuous.of_at
and 5 lemmas of Mathlib ContinuousAt.tendsto, Filter.Tendsto.squeeze, Units.norm_pos, LE.le.trans, div_le_iff₀'
. ∎
Theorem 104.11src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: norm_inclMatrix_le_one
Let $m$ and $n$ be finite types. Let $e$ be an injective function from $n$ to $m$. Then $\NormnorminMACRoFCP {(\inclMatrixMACRoEAJ {\ComplexMACRoAM}{e})}\leq 1$.
Proof. By inclMatrix_gram_eq_one
and 4 lemmas of Mathlib IsStarProjection.norm_le, le_of_sq_le_sq, Matrix.l2_opNorm_conjTranspose_mul_self, sq
. ∎
Theorem 104.12src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: norm_J₁
Let $x$ be a shape. Then $\NormnorminMACRoFCP {\ShapeJuMACRoCQW {x}}= 1$.
Proof. By IsHermInvolution.mem_unitary
and 1 lemma of Mathlib CStarRing.norm_of_mem_unitary
. ∎
Theorem 104.13src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: norm_le_of_mem_frameBox
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $x_{1} \in \SubtypeSet {x_{10}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{10}})}$. Let $M \in \Real$ such that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\frameBoxMACRoDVY {M}}{x_{1}}$, $1 \leq M$ and for all elements $a$ of $\FinMACRoFDI {\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}}$, $\NormnormMACRoAK {\mathrm{WithLp\_\_toLp} (2 , \lambda x_{5} . \UnitsvalappMACRoHCH {\InvinvMACRoFW {x_{1}}}{x_{5}}{\eStarMACRoDRS {a}})}\leq M$. Then $\NormnorminMACRoFCP {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {x_{1}}}+ 1 \leq 8 \times (\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)\times M$.
Let x in G₁ have all |x⁻¹f_i| ≤ M and all |x⁻¹e*_a| ≤ M, with 1 ≤ M. Then ‖x‖ + 1 ≤ 8(ϱ+1)M.
Proof. By norm_J₁, Shape.middleGram, Shape.Form.splitGram, U.coe_inv, U, Shape.J₁, frameBox, frameVector, U.norm_inv, IsHermInvolution, IsHermInvolution.mul_self, U.one_le_norm, Shape.BesselIndex
and 20 lemmas of Mathlib add_mul, PiLp.ext, norm_sub_le, mul_assoc, Matrix.mul_assoc, norm_le_insert', Matrix.mul_apply, norm_add_le, sub_mul, Matrix.mulVec_eq_sum, CStarRing.norm_self_mul_star, LT.lt.trans_le, norm_mul_le, mul_le_mul_iff_left₀, one_pos, norm_sum_le, ContinuousLinearMap.ext, one_le_mul_of_one_le_of_one_le, InnerProductSpace.norm_rankOne, Fin.forall_fin_succ'
. ∎
Theorem 104.14src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: norm_inner_J₁_le
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $v$ and $w$ be Euclidean spaces of $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $\ShapeBesselIndexMACRoCQI {x}$. Then $\NormnormMACRoAK {\mathrm{Inner\_\_inner} (\ComplexMACRoAM , v , \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\mathrm{Matrix\_\_toEuclideanLin}}{\ShapeJuMACRoCQW {x}})}\leq \NormnormMACRoAK {v}\times \NormnormMACRoAK {w}$.
Proof. By norm_J₁
and 3 lemmas of Mathlib Matrix.l2_opNorm_mulVec, one_mul, norm_inner_le_norm
. ∎
Theorem 104.15src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: realFrameGram_eq_inner
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $x_{1} \in \SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{5}})}$. Let $y' \in \SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{4}})}$. Let $a' , b \in \FinMACRoFDI {(\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)}$. Then $\realFrameGramappMACRoGRZ {x_{1}}{a'}{b}$ is equal to $\mathrm{Inner\_\_inner} (\ComplexMACRoAM , \frameVectorMACRoDWI {y'}{a'}, \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\mathrm{Matrix\_\_toEuclideanLin}}{\ShapeJuMACRoCQW {x}})$.
Proof.
By 2 lemmas of Mathlib mul_assoc, dotProduct_comm
. ∎
Theorem 104.16src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: realFrameGram_mul_invariant
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $x_{1} \in \SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{6}})}$. Let $s \in \SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {(\ShapemiddleGramMACRoCRR {x}{1})}}{x_{5}})}$ such that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\MaxmaxMACRoEC {\ShapeFormbesselUMACRoCQP}{\ShapeFormbesselHMACRoCQM}}{s}$. Then $\realFrameGramMACRoEOW {(s \times x_{1})}$ is equal to $\realFrameGramMACRoEOW {x_{1}}$ and $\realFrameGramMACRoEOW {(x_{1} \times s)}$ is equal to $\realFrameGramMACRoEOW {x_{1}}$.
Proof. By realFrameGram_eq_inner, frameVector_mul
and 2 lemmas of Mathlib mul_assoc, Matrix.ext_iff
. ∎
Theorem 104.17src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: realFrameGram_eq_frameMatrix_mul
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $x_{1} \in \SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{5}})}$. Let $s \in \SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {(\ShapemiddleGramMACRoCRR {x}{1})}}{x_{4}})}$ such that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormbesselUMACRoCQO}{s}$. Then $\realFrameGramMACRoEOW {x_{1}}$ is equal to the product of the conjugate transpose of $\frameMatrixMACRoDWF {s}$ and $\realFrameGramMACRoEOW {(s \times x_{1})}$.
Proof. By mem_besselU0_iff, frameGram_inv_mul_mul
and 1 lemma of Mathlib OneMemClass.one_mem
. ∎
Theorem 104.18src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: norm_realFrameGram_le_mul
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $x_{1} \in \SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{5}})}$. Let $y' \in \SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{4}})}$. Let $a' , b \in \FinMACRoFDI {(\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)}$. Then $\NormnormMACRoAK {(\realFrameGramappMACRoGRZ {x_{1}}{a'}{b})}\leq \NormnormMACRoAK {(\frameVectorMACRoDWI {y'}{a'})}\times \NormnormMACRoAK {(\frameVectorMACRoDWI {(\InvinvMACRoFW {x_{1}}\times y')}{b})}$.
Proof. By norm_inner_J₁_le, realFrameGram_eq_inner. ∎
Theorem 104.19src/New/RealPlace/FrameNorm.lean: besselSize_le_of_dual
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $x_{1} \in \SubtypeSet {x_{3}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeGuMACRoCQV {x}}{x_{3}})}$. Let $M \in \Real$ such that $1 \leq M$. Let $V$ be a function from elements of $\Natural$ to Euclidean spaces of $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $\ShapeBesselIndexMACRoCQI {x}$ such that for all elements $a_{2}$ of $\FinMACRoFDI {(\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)}$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\frameVectorMACRoDWI {x_{1}}{a_{2}}}{V (a_{2})}$, for all $a_{1} \leq 2 \times \ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}$ in $\Natural$, $\NormnormMACRoAK {V (a_{1})}\leq M$ and for all elements $a$ and $b$ of $\Natural$, if $a + b \leq 2 \times \ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}$, then $\EqtMACRoDE {\mathrm{Inner\_\_inner} (\ComplexMACRoAM , V (a), \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\mathrm{Matrix\_\_toEuclideanLin}}{\ShapeJuMACRoCQW {x}})}{\iteMACRoDD {\mathrm{Eq} (a + b , 2 \times \ShapeufMACRoFEK {x})}{\NegnegMACRoFV {1}}{0}}$. Then $\besselSizeMACRoDKX {x_{1}}\leq 8 \times (\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)\times M$.
Let V_0, …, V_{2ϱ} have lengths at most M ≥ 1, with V_a = x⁻¹f_{a+1} for a ≤ ϱ, and h(V_a, V_b) = −1 if a+b = 2ϱ, 0 if a+b < 2ϱ. Then `besselSize` x ≤ 8(ϱ+1)M.
Proof. By norm_inner_J₁_le, Shape.n, mem_unitriGroup_of_mem_besselU, realFrameGram_eq_inner, norm_le_of_mem_frameBox, unipotentOf_mem_besselU0, besselSize_besselS_mul, Shape.ϱ, liftUnitri_mem_besselU, besselSize_le, frameVector_eq_sum, Shape.J₁, liftUnitri, frameVector, frameMatrix_unitriangular, Shape.Form.frameGram, IsHermInvolution, IsHermInvolution.mul_self, frameVector_mul, mul_mul_eq_of_mul_eq, exists_mul_unipotentOf_dualCols_eq, mul_inclMatrix, unitriMatrix
and 29 lemmas of Mathlib Matrix.l2_opNorm_mulVec, inner_sum, norm_smul, Matrix.mul_assoc, sum_inner, InnerProductSpace.gramSchmidt_orthogonal, ne_of_gt, Subgroup.mem_sup_left, Eq.trans_le, sq, add_comm, Finset.sum_eq_single, inner_add_right, InvMemClass.inv_mem, le_of_mul_le_mul_left, inner_smul_right, InnerProductSpace.gramSchmidt_def'', inner_eq_zero_symm, lt_or_ge, LE.le.trans, Finset.mem_Iio, Finset.filter_gt_eq_Iio, dotProduct_comm, Matrix.ext, Matrix.one_mul, Finset.sum_ite, one_mul, inner_smul_left, norm_inner_le_norm
. ∎

New.RealPlace.GramSectionBounds

Definition 104.20src/New/RealPlace/GramSectionBounds.lean: invCorner
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $Z \in \ShapeFrameMatMACRoCQU {x}$. Then $\invCornerMACRoEBV {Z}$ is a complex defined as $\InvinvappMACRoGSM {Z}{\ShapetopMACRoCRX {x}}{\ShapetopMACRoCRX {x}}$.
For a complex n×n matrix Z, n = ϱ + 1 the length of the frame, the last diagonal entry ξ of Z⁻¹.
Definition 104.21src/New/RealPlace/GramSectionBounds.lean: Shape.openCell
Let $x$ be a shape. Then $\ShapeopenCellMACRoCRU$ is an element of $\wp \ShapeFrameMatMACRoCQU {x}$ defined as $\SubtypeSet {Z}{\mathrm{and} (\mathrm{Neq} (\MatrixdetMACRoGY {Z}, 0), \mathrm{Lt} (\NormnormMACRoAK {\invCornerMACRoEBV {Z}}, 1))}$.
The set Ω of invertible complex (ϱ+1)×(ϱ+1) matrices Z such that the last diagonal entry ξ of Z⁻¹ satisfies |ξ| < 1.
Definition 104.22src/New/RealPlace/GramSectionBounds.lean: cornerDefect
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $Z \in \ShapeFrameMatMACRoCQU {x}$. Then $\cornerDefectMACRoDPA {Z}$ is a real number defined as $1 - \NormnormMACRoAK {\invCornerMACRoEBV {Z}}^ {2}$.
For a (ϱ+1)×(ϱ+1) complex matrix Z, the real number 1 − |ξ|², ξ the last diagonal entry of Z⁻¹.
Definition 104.23src/New/RealPlace/GramSectionBounds.lean: IsBoundedInCell
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $Z \in \ShapeFrameMatMACRoCQU {x}$. Let $P \in \Real$. Then $\IsBoundedInCellMACRoBFE {Z}{P}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that $Z$ is an element of $\ShapeopenCellMACRoCRU$, $\NormnormMACRoAK {\InvinvMACRoFW {Z}}\leq P$, $\NormnormMACRoAK {Z}\leq P$ and $\InvinvMACRoFW {\RealsqrtMACRoHU {\cornerDefectMACRoDPA {Z}}}\leq P$.
Z lies in Ω (`Shape.openCell`), and ‖Z‖, ‖Z⁻¹‖ (operator norms) and δ^{−1/2} are at most P, where δ = 1 − |ξ|², ξ the last diagonal entry of Z⁻¹.
Definition 104.24src/New/RealPlace/GramSectionBounds.lean: diagLast
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $x_{1}$ be a complex. Then $\diagLastMACRoDRD {x_{1}}$ is an element of $\ShapeFrameMatMACRoCQU {x}$ defined as the diagonal of $\mathrm{Fin\_\_snoc} (1 , x_{1})$.
For a complex number x, the (ϱ+1)×(ϱ+1) diagonal matrix diag(1, …, 1, x).
Definition 104.25src/New/RealPlace/GramSectionBounds.lean: sectionCoeff
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $Z \in \ShapeFrameMatMACRoCQU {x}$. Let $W$ be an element of $\mathrm{optParam}$ applied to the set of matrixes of $\FinMACRoFDI {(\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)}$ and $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and the conjugate transpose of $Z$. Then $\sectionCoeffMACRoESD {Z}{W}$ is an element of $\ShapeFrameMatMACRoCQU {x}$ defined as $\diagLastMACRoDRD {0}- 1 - \diagLastMACRoDRD {0}\times \InvinvMACRoFW {W}\times (1 - \diagLastMACRoDRD {\InvinvMACRoFW {2}})\times \InvinvMACRoFW {Z}\times \diagLastMACRoDRD {2}$.
For Z and W (by default Zᴴ), the matrix with last row (0, …, 0, −1) and other entries −(W⁻¹)_{a,last}(Z⁻¹)_{last,b}, halved unless b is last.
Definition 104.26src/New/RealPlace/GramSectionBounds.lean: sectionFrame
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $Z \in \ShapeFrameMatMACRoCQU {x}$. Let $W$ be an element of $\mathrm{optParam}$ applied to the set of matrixes of $\FinMACRoFDI {(\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)}$ and $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and the conjugate transpose of $Z$. Let $s$ be an element of $\mathrm{optParam}$ applied to $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and the square root of $1 - \invCornerMACRoEBV {Z}\times \invCornerMACRoEBV {W}$. Then $\sectionFrameMACRoESE {Z}{W}{s}$ is a matrix of $\FinMACRoFDI {(\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)}$ and $\ComplexMACRoAM$ defined as $(\ShapeframeColsMACRoCRM {x}\times \sectionCoeffMACRoESD {Z}{W}+ \ShapedualFrameColsMACRoCRL {x}\times \diagLastMACRoDRD {s})\times Z$.
The matrix (F·Y + F'·diag(1, …, 1, s))·Z, with F the frame, F' = (e*₁, …, e*_ϱ, x_{k₀}), Y = `sectionCoeff`; its columns are the images of the frame under γ_Z.
Definition 104.27src/New/RealPlace/GramSectionBounds.lean: sectionMatrix
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $Z \in \ShapeFrameMatMACRoCQU {x}$. Let $W$ be an element of $\mathrm{optParam}$ applied to the set of matrixes of $\FinMACRoFDI {(\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)}$ and $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and the conjugate transpose of $Z$. Let $s$ be an element of $\mathrm{optParam}$ applied to $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and the square root of $1 - \invCornerMACRoEBV {Z}\times \invCornerMACRoEBV {W}$. Then $\sectionMatrixMACRoESH {Z}{W}{s}$ is a matrix of $\ShapeBesselIndexMACRoCQI {x}$ and $\ComplexMACRoAM$ defined as $1 + (\sectionFrameMACRoESE {Z}{W}{\mathrm{Complex\_\_sqrt} (1 - \invCornerMACRoEBV {Z}\times \invCornerMACRoEBV {W})}- \ShapeframeColsMACRoCRM {x})\times \mathrm{Matrix\_\_conjTranspose} (\ShapeframeColsMACRoCRM {x})+ ((\ShapeframeColsMACRoCRM {x}\times \InvinvMACRoFW {W}- \sectionFrameMACRoESE {Z}{W}{\mathrm{Complex\_\_sqrt} (1 - \invCornerMACRoEBV {Z}\times \invCornerMACRoEBV {W})}\times \sectionCoeffMACRoESD {W}{Z})\times \diagLastMACRoDRD {\InvinvMACRoFW {s}}- \ShapedualFrameColsMACRoCRL {x})\times \mathrm{Matrix\_\_conjTranspose} (\ShapedualFrameColsMACRoCRL {x})$.
The matrix 1 − FFᴴ − F'F'ᴴ + SFᴴ + (FW⁻¹ − S·Y(W, Z))·diag(1, …, 1, s⁻¹)·F'ᴴ, with S = `sectionFrame`, Y = `sectionCoeff`; by default W = Zᴴ, s = √δ.
Theorem 104.28src/New/RealPlace/GramSectionBounds.lean: gramDiscReal_eq
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $Z \in \ShapeFrameMatMACRoCQU {x}$ such that $\MatrixdetMACRoGY {Z}\neq 0$. Then $\gramDiscRealMACRoDXX {Z}= \NormnormMACRoAK {\MatrixdetMACRoGY {Z}}^ {2}\times \cornerDefectMACRoDPA {Z}$.
Proof. By adjugate_fin_succ_eq_det_submatrix'. ∎
Theorem 104.29src/New/RealPlace/GramSectionBounds.lean: mem_openCell_iff
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $Z \in \ShapeFrameMatMACRoCQU {x}$. Then $Z$ is an element of $\ShapeopenCellMACRoCRU$, if and only if $0 < \gramDiscRealMACRoDXX {Z}$.
Proof. By gramDiscReal_eq. ∎
Theorem 104.30src/New/RealPlace/GramSectionBounds.lean: continuous_gramDiscReal
Let $x$ be a shape. Then $\gramDiscRealpartMACRoGSC$ is continuous.
Proof. By leadingMinor, frameDisc. ∎
Theorem 104.31src/New/RealPlace/GramSectionBounds.lean: isOpen_openCell
Let $x$ be a shape. Then $\ShapeopenCellMACRoCRU$ is open.
The open cell, the domain of the double coset section, is an open set.
Proof. By mem_openCell_iff, continuous_gramDiscReal
and 3 lemmas of Mathlib Set.ext, isOpen_Ioi, IsOpen.preimage
. ∎
Theorem 104.32src/New/RealPlace/GramSectionBounds.lean: differentiableAt_sectionMatrix_uncurry
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $Z \in \ShapeFrameMatMACRoCQU {x}$. Let $W$ be an element of $\mathrm{optParam}$ applied to the set of matrixes of $\FinMACRoFDI {(\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)}$ and $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and the conjugate transpose of $Z$. Assume that $Z$ is an element of $\ShapeopenCellMACRoCRU$. Assume that $W$ is an element of $\ShapeopenCellMACRoCRU$. Then $\mathrm{Function\_\_uncurry} (\lambda x_{1} . \lambda x_{2} . \sectionMatrixMACRoESH {x_{1}}{x_{2}}{\mathrm{Complex\_\_sqrt} (1 - \invCornerMACRoEBV {x_{1}}\times \invCornerMACRoEBV {x_{2}})})$ is differentiable at $\ProdmkMACRoDR {Z}{W}$.
Proof. By diagLast, sectionCoeff, sectionMatrix, sectionFrame, invCorner

. ∎

Theorem 104.36src/New/RealPlace/GramSectionBounds.lean: norm_sectionMatrix_le

Theorem 104.52src/New/RealPlace/CellNeighbourhood.lean: openCellChart_injOn

Theorem 104.53src/New/RealPlace/CellNeighbourhood.lean: mem_openCell_and_bounds_of_near

New.RealPlace.Size

Structure 104.56src/New/RealPlace/Size.lean: Size
Let $A$ be a set. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $T \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x_{2}}{A}$. Then $\SizeMACRoCSL {T}$ is a set.
A size: sets B_i ∋ 0, 1 of A, stable under − and involution, B_iB_j ⊂ B_{i+j}, sums of ≤ rank+1 terms of B_i in B_{i+1}, Gram matrix and inverse in B₁.
Definition 104.57src/New/RealPlace/Size.lean: Size.ofSize
Let $A$ be a set. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $T \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x_{2}}{A}$. Let $\mathrm{self} \in \SizeMACRoCSL {T}$. Let $x_{3} \in \Natural$. Then $\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {\mathrm{self}}{x_{3}}$ is an element of $\wp A$.
Theorem 104.58src/New/RealPlace/Size.lean: Size.mul_mem_ofSize
Let $A$ be a commutative ring such that $A$ is a star ring. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $T \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x_{2}}{A}$. Let $\mathrm{self} \in \SizeMACRoCSL {T}$. Let $i , j \in \Natural$. Let $x_{3} , y \in A$ such that $x_{3}$ is an element of $\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {\mathrm{self}}{i}$ and $y$ is an element of $\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {\mathrm{self}}{j}$. Then $x_{3} \times y$ is an element of $\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {\mathrm{self}}{(i + j)}$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎

Assume that for all elements $a_{1}$ of $n$, $f (a_{1})$ is an element of $\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {\mathrm{self}}{i}$. Then $\FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{a}{f (a)}$ is an element of $\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {\mathrm{self}}{(i + 1)}$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Theorem 104.60src/New/RealPlace/Size.lean: Size.neg_star_mem_ofSize
Let $A$ be a commutative ring such that $A$ is a star ring. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $T \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x_{2}}{A}$. Let $\mathrm{self} \in \SizeMACRoCSL {T}$. Let $i \in \Natural$. Let $x_{3} \in A$. Then the negation of $x_{3}$ is an element of $\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {\mathrm{self}}{i}$, if and only if $x_{3}$ is an element of $\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {\mathrm{self}}{i}$ and the star of $x_{3}$ is an element of $\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {\mathrm{self}}{i}$, if and only if $x_{3}$ is an element of $\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {\mathrm{self}}{i}$.

Definition 104.63src/New/RealPlace/Size.lean: weylRatio
Let $A$ be a set. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $T \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x_{2}}{A}$. Let $j \in \Natural$. Let $p$ and $q$ be functions from $\Natural$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {A}$. The element w_q⁻¹w_p of the unitary group of V over A, for the Weyl elements w_p, w_q of length j.

and 3 lemmas of Mathlib Matrix.mul_apply, division_def, Matrix.ext

Theorem 104.65src/New/RealPlace/Size.lean: card_succ_le_floorOf
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $a \in \FinMACRoFDI {\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}}$. Then $\FintypecardMACRoIR {(\SubtypeSet {i}{\mathrm{Leq} (a + 1 , \floorOfMACRoDVJ {i})})}= \ShapekMACRoCRP {x}+ 1 + 2 \times (\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}- (a + 1))$.
For a < ϱ, the number of basis indices of floor (`floorOf`) at least a + 1 is k + 1 + 2(ϱ − (a + 1)), the rank of the orthogonal complement of the first a + 1 hyperbolic planes.
Proof.
By 3 lemmas of Mathlib Fintype.card_congr, Finset.filter_lt_eq_Ioi, Fintype.card_subtype
. ∎
Definition 104.66src/New/RealPlace/Size.lean: Size.vecsOfSize
Let $A$ and $n$ be sets. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $T \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x_{2}}{A}$. Let $S \in \SizeMACRoCSL {T}$. Let $i \in \Natural$. Then $\SizevecsOfSizeMACRoCSS {i}$ is an element of the power set of the set of functions from $n$ to $A$ defined as $\SubtypeSet {v}{\mathrm{forall} (n , \lambda a . \MembershipmemMACRoDQ {(\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {S}{i})}{v (a)})}$.
The vector v has size i: all its entries lie in B_i.
Definition 104.67src/New/RealPlace/Size.lean: Size.HasMatSize
Let $A$, $l$ and $n$ be sets. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $T \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x_{2}}{A}$. Let $S \in \SizeMACRoCSL {T}$. Let $i \in \Natural$. Let $X$ be a matrix of $n$ and $A$. Then $\SizeHasMatSizeMACRoCSM {i}{X}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that for all elements $a$ of $l$, for all elements $b$ of $n$, $X (a , b)$ is an element of $\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {S}{i}$.
The matrix X has size i: all its entries lie in B_i.
Definition 104.68src/New/RealPlace/Size.lean: Size.IsHead

Then $\SizeIsHeadMACRoCSO {j}{p}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that for all $l \leq j$ in $\Natural$, $\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\InvinvMACRoFW {(\FinsetProd {\FinsetrangeMACRoDK {l}}{a}{p (a)})}}$ is an element of $\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {S}{1}$.
The units p form a head of length j for the size S: the inverse of every product D_l = p₁⋯p_l, l ≤ j, has size 1.

Theorem 104.81src/New/RealPlace/Size.lean: Size.row_mem_ofSize_of_dual_frame
Let $A$ be a commutative ring. Let $l$ be a set. Assume that $A$ is a star ring. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $T \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x_{2}}{A}$. Let $c , d , j , i \in \Natural$. Let $S \in \SizeMACRoCSL {T}$. Let $X$ be a matrix of $\ShapeBesselIndexMACRoCQI {x_{2}}$ and $A$. Let $m \in l$. Assume that for all $a < j$ in $\FinMACRoFDI {\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x_{2}}}$, there exists a function $w$ from $\ShapeBesselIndexMACRoCQI {x_{2}}$ to $A$, such that for all $b \leq a$ in $\FinMACRoFDI {\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x_{2}}}$, $\EqtMACRoDE {w (\eStarMACRoDRS {b})}{\iteMACRoDD {(\EqtMACRoDE {b}{a})}{1}{0}}$, $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\SizevecsOfSizeMACRoCSS {c}}{w}$ and $\mathrm{Matrix\_\_mulVec} (w)$ is an element of $\SizevecsOfSizeMACRoCSS {d}$. Assume that for all elements $k_{1}$ of $\ShapeBesselIndexMACRoCQI {x_{2}}$, if $j \leq \floorOfMACRoDVJ {k_{1}}$ or $\FIlevelMACRoAKA {k_{1}}\leq \ShapeufMACRoFEK {x_{2}}$, then $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {S}{d}}{X (m , k_{1})}$. Let $k \in \ShapeBesselIndexMACRoCQI {x_{2}}$ such that $j \leq \floorOfMACRoDVJ {k}+ i$ or $\FIlevelMACRoAKA {k}\leq \ShapeufMACRoFEK {x_{2}}$. Let $A$ be a commutative ring such that $A$ is a star ring. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $T \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x_{2}}{A}$. Let $c , j \in \Natural$. Let $A$ and $I$ be sets. Let $x_{5}$ be a shape. Let $T \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x_{5}}{A}$. Let $m , x_{6} , x_{7} \in \Natural$. Let $F \in \LevelMACRoBRH {I}{T}{m}$. The convolution (η_{l+i−1}∗θ_{l+i−1}) ∗ ⋯ ∗ (η_l∗θ_l) of i factors; for l = 0 it is the measure 𝗆_i.

Theorem 104.129src/New/RealPlace/LevelGauge.lean: Level.lintegral_mul_left_eq_of_factor
Let $A$ be a commutative ring such that $A$ is a star ring, $A$ is a topological space, $A$ is a measure space and $\PlaceMACRoCKW {A}$. Let $I$ be a finite type. Let $x_{5}$ be a shape. Let $\rho$ be a measure of $\SubtypeSet {x_{39}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {T}}{x_{39}})}$. Assume that $\nu$ is finite. Assume that $\rho$ is finite. Let $s \in \SubtypeSet {x_{34}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {T}}{x_{34}})}$. Let $S$ be a subgroup of $\SubtypeSet {x_{33}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {T}}{x_{33}})}$. Assume that for all elements $s_{1}$ of $\SubtypeSet {x_{22}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {T}}{x_{22}})}$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {S}{s_{1}}$, then $\mathrm{MeasureTheory\_\_Measure\_\_mconv}$ applied to $\rho$ and the Dirac of $s_{1}$ is equal to $\rho$. Assume that $\mathrm{MeasureTheory\_\_Measure\_\_mconv}$ applied to the Dirac of $s$ and $\rho$ is equal to $\mathrm{MeasureTheory\_\_Measure\_\_mconv}$ applied to $\rho$ and the Dirac of $s$. Let 𝖬 = ρ ∗ ν be a Haar measure, with ρ carried by the subgroup S, right S-invariant and commuting with the unit mass at s. Then ∫ Φ(sg) dν(g) = ∫ Φ dν for measurable left S-invariant Φ ≥ 0.

and 11 lemmas of Mathlib MeasureTheory.Measure.mconv_assoc, MeasureTheory.map_mul_left_eq_self, mul_assoc, ENNReal.div_mul_cancel, LT.lt.ne', MeasureTheory.Measure.dirac_mconv, ENNReal.top_ne_zero, MeasureTheory.lintegral_const_mul, LT.lt.ne, MeasureTheory.ae_lt_top, MeasureTheory.exists_pos_lintegral_lt_of_sigmaFinite
. ∎

Theorem 104.163src/New/RealPlace/FlatCharts.lean: Level.upperMeasure_isNear_le

(\FloorPairsMACRoAPY {b}{m}, \mathrm{Sum} (I , \FloorPairsMACRoAPY {b}{m})))\rightarrow A$, such that there exists a function $\mu$ from $\ProdMACRoEZ {A}{(\mathrm{Elem} (\FloorPairsMACRoAPY {b}{m})\rightarrow \mathrm{Elem} (A))}$ to $\ENNRealMACRoCG$, such that $\zeta$ is finite, $\mathrm{MeasureTheory\_\_MeasurePreserving}$ holds for $k$, the product of the product of the volume and the volume and $\zeta$ and $\rho$, the map of the function that maps $x_{20}$ to $\mathrm{Matrix\_\_mulVec} (x_{20})$ and $\rho$ is equal to $q$, for all elements $r$ of $\ProdMACRoEZ {(\ProdMACRoEZ {(\ProdMACRoEZ {A}{(\mathrm{Elem} (\FloorPairsMACRoAPY {b}{m})\rightarrow \mathrm{Elem} (A))})}{(\mathrm{Elem} (I)\rightarrow \mathrm{Elem} (A))})}{Z}$, $\ProdfstMACRoDW {r}$ is equal to Structure 104.204src/New/RealPlace/ComplexGauge.lean: RealPlaceSizes
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $\rho , b \in \Real$. Let $s \in \Natural$. Then $\RealPlaceSizesMACRoCOH {x}{\rho}{s}{b}$ is a proposition.
The numerical hypotheses at a real place: ρ = b^(6n+2) ≥ 8r with b ≥ 2, |κ| = s + 3n + 2 ≥ n⁷, s > 10⁹n³ and ϱ ≥ 10⁸. Here ϱ counts the hyperbolic planes of the `Shape`, n = ϱ + 1, κ indexes the basis of the summand X₀ and r is the rank of the whole space.

Structure 104.237src/New/RealPlace/LocalTheorem.lean: Place.IsBessel

Then $\sieveVectorMACRoEST {F}{p}$ is a Euclidean space of $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $\ProdMACRoEZ {I}{I}$ defined as $\normCorrelationMACRoEIJ {(\cellChartMACRoDMG {x}{\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}}{\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {p (\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x})}})}{F}{p}$.
The vector Ξ(p) = ω(p)J_F(γ_p) (`normCorrelation`), with γ_p = w_{(p₁,…,p_ϱ)}·w_ϱ(p_n/min(1, |p_n|)), n = ϱ+1, and w_ϱ the boost `cellChart`.
